Result: 实验表明，多视图预测显著优于单视图方法。置信图的加权显著提高了先验的效果。在标准基准测试中，重建质量得到持续改善，尤其是在复杂场景（包括镜面物体）中取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 将几何先验集成到3DGS框架中，并结合多视图预测及其置信图加权，是提升新型视图合成中几何重建质量的有效方法，特别适用于包含高镜面反射物体的复杂场景。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a widely-used tool for novel view synthesis, offering real-time rendering in a sparse representation. However, the method's reliance on structure-from-motion initialization and photometric optimization can lead to suboptimal geometric reconstruction, particularly for objects with high specularity. In this work, we investigate the integration of geometric priors, in the form of predicted normal and depth maps, into the 3DGS framework to improve the reconstruction quality. We analyze the effect of incorporating these priors into GS-based methods and our evaluation reveals that multi-view predictions, as they are done by the recent visual geometry grounded transformer (VGGT), outperform single-view alternatives. A major factor is the existence of a confidence map for the estimations, which comes as a by-product of multi-view models and which can significantly improve the effectiveness of priors by weighting each prediction appropriately. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show consistent improvement in reconstruction quality and significant gains in complex scenes including specular objects.

</details>


### [92] [Sample-Adaptive Latent Rewards for Uncertainty-Guided Diffusion Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06125)
*Rui Li,Yuanzhi Liang,Ke Hao,Ziqiao Weng,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,XueLong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SURE是一个用于图像和视频扩散模型的统一潜在空间框架，它通过学习奖励分布并利用其不确定性来指导模型对齐，解决了现有潜在奖励模型因缺乏不确定性估计而导致的优化不稳定和奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在奖励模型仅输出标量奖励分数，无法评估其预测的不确定性。这导致生成器无法区分可靠与不可靠的反馈，可能引发优化方向错误和奖励黑客现象。本文旨在解决这一缺陷，提高基于人类偏好的对齐效率和稳定性。

Method: 本文提出了SURE框架，包含两个核心组件：1) SURE-LRM：一个样本自适应的潜在奖励模型，它为每个含噪潜在预测高斯分布效用（均值预测奖励，方差反映预测不确定性）。2) SURE-REFL：一种不确定性引导的奖励反馈学习方法，用于密集后训练。它在去噪轨迹的关键转换点查询冻结的SURE-LRM，将预测方差转换为可靠性权重，对同一转换点的样本进行加权奖励反馈，并通过局部转换进行反向传播。整个过程保持在潜在空间，无需解码到像素空间或完整的去噪计算图。

Result: 实验表明，SURE-LRM在偏好预测任务上优于强大的基线模型。SURE-REFL在各项指标上达到了最先进的性能，并显著提高了优化稳定性。在评估的方法中，SURE-REFL在VBench的质量、语义和总分上均取得了最高分。

Conclusion: SURE框架通过引入和利用奖励预测的不确定性估计，成功提升了视觉扩散模型与人类偏好对齐的效率和稳定性。它提供了一种无需像素空间解码的密集监督信号，实现了更优的生成质量与优化过程。

Abstract: Latent reward models can supervise visual diffusion models without decoding intermediate states into pixel space. This makes alignment with human preferences more efficient. However, existing latent reward models output only scalar scores. They do not estimate the uncertainty of each prediction. The generator therefore cannot determine which feedback is reliable. This can drive optimization in the wrong direction and lead to reward hacking. We propose \textsc{SURE}, a unified latent-space framework for image and video diffusion models. It learns reward distributions and directly uses their reliability to guide dense post-training. First, we propose sample-adaptive latent reward model (\textsc{SURE-LRM}). It predicts a Gaussian utility for each noisy latent. Its mean predicts the reward score. Its variance reflect the uncertainty of prediction without human annotation. The learned distribution then guides post-training through uncertainty-guided reward feedback learning (\textsc{SURE-REFL}). This method provides uncertainty-guided dense feedback along the denoising trajectory. At selected transitions, \textsc{SURE-REFL} queries the frozen \textsc{SURE-LRM}. It converts detached variance into reliability weights for samples at the same transition. Each weighted reward is backpropagated only through its local transition. The entire process remains in latent space and requires neither pixel-space decoding nor the full denoising graph. Experiments show that \textsc{SURE-LRM} improves preference prediction over strong baselines. \textsc{SURE-REFL} achieves the sota performance among various metrics and further improves optimization stability. It also achieves the highest VBench quality, semantic, and total scores among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [93] [Patient Pose Assessment Using a CT-Based Framework for Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06126)
*Manuel Laufer,Dominik Mairhöfer,Malte Sieren,Hauke Gerdes,Fabio Leal dos Reis,Arpad Bischof,Thomas Käster,Erhardt Barth,Jörg Barkhausen,Thomas Martinetz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种从CT扫描合成生成深度图像和对应X光片的框架，用于训练AI模型自动评估X光摄影中的患者姿势。在合成数据集上预训练后，模型在真实上踝关节数据上的姿势评估性能提升了多达11个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者姿势是决定X光诊断质量的关键因素，但人工摆位困难且缺乏标准化。基于深度图像的AI自动评估可提供帮助，但因监管限制，获取配对的真实深度图像和X光片数据在实践中面临巨大挑战，阻碍了模型训练。

Method: 1. 开发一个从计算机断层扫描（CT）合成生成配对数据（深度图像与X光片）的框架。2. 构建一个包含3077对上踝关节图像的合成数据集。3. 在该合成数据集上对AI模型进行预训练，随后用其对真实上踝关节的姿势进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用所提出的合成数据进行预训练，能够有效提升模型在真实数据上的表现。对于上踝关节的姿势评估任务，模型的性能提升了高达11个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地通过合成数据生成方法，解决了医疗影像AI领域中高质量配对训练数据难以获取的瓶颈问题。所提出的框架和合成数据集显著提升了患者姿势评估AI模型的性能，证明了合成数据在应对监管和数据稀缺挑战时的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: An adequate diagnostic quality of radiographs is essential for reliable diagnoses and treatment planning. The patient's pose during radiography is one of the most important factors determining the diagnostic quality. Since patient positioning is difficult and not standardized, an automated AI-based approach using depth images to automatically assess the patient's pose before the radiograph has been taken would be helpful. Due to regulatory hurdles, however, it is difficult in practice to acquire the required depth images and corresponding radiographs. In this paper, we present a framework that can generate such training data synthetically from Computed Tomography scans. We further show that by pretraining on our generated synthetic dataset consisting of 3077 image pairs of upper ankle joints, the pose assessment of real upper ankle joints can be improved by up to 11 percentage points.

</details>


### [94] [Learning visual representations for compositional analysis of artworks and photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06142)
*Fatemeh Behrad,Tinne Tuytelaars,Johan Wagemans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种分析视觉构图的方法：受人类感知启发的对象-图注意力模型与基于大规模数据微调的基础模型。结果表明，在数据充足时微调模型表现更优，但可解释性和泛化性下降；而人类启发方法在保持可解释性的同时仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构图是视觉艺术中传达意义、情感和美学的核心维度，但仍是视觉理解中形式化程度最低的方面之一。现有工作指出，学习有意义的构图表示存在持续差距，并将此归因于语义偏差，认为人类启发的方法可能是解决这一问题的关键。

Method: 本文并行比较了两种范式：1）人类启发方法，基于感知分组，使用对象中心模型进行区域级分解，并利用图注意力网络捕捉元素间空间关系；2）微调基础模型，借助近期出现的大规模构图数据集进行训练。两种方法在三项任务上进行评估：构图评分/类别预测、构图图像检索和视觉显著性检测。

Result: 当使用冻结编码器时，人类启发方法在保持可解释性的同时，取得了与基线模型相当的性能。然而，当有足够数据进行微调时，大型自监督模型的性能显著超越人类启发方法，但代价是可解释性和跨领域泛化能力的降低。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在构图分析中，模型性能与可解释性/泛化性之间的权衡。人类启发方法提供了可解释且泛化性较好的解决方案，而数据驱动的微调模型在数据充足时可达到更高的性能。这为未来根据具体应用场景（如需要解释性或跨领域部署）选择合适方法提供了参考。

Abstract: Composition, the deliberate arrangement of visual elements, is central to how meaning, emotion, and aesthetic quality are conveyed in artwork, yet it remains among the least formalized dimensions of visual understanding. Prior work highlights a persistent gap in learning meaningful compositional representations, attributing it to semantic bias and suggesting that human-inspired approaches may be key. We compare two parallel paradigms for composition analysis: a human-inspired method grounded in perceptual grouping, and fine-tuned foundation models enabled by recent large-scale compositional datasets. The human-inspired approach uses object-centric models for region-level decomposition and a graph attention network to capture spatial relationships between elements. Both paradigms are evaluated on composition score/category prediction, compositional image retrieval, and visual saliency detection. With frozen encoders, the human-inspired method achieves competitive performance while remaining interpretable. When sufficient data enables fine-tuning, large self-supervised models outperform significantly, but at the cost of interpretability and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [95] [BendTwin: Robust Dense-to-Sparse Physical Reconstruction with Bending-Aware Differentiable Spring-Mass Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06164)
*Yixiong Jing,Qi Wang,Lin Chen,Junwei Jiang,Guangming Wang,Haibing Wu,Olaf Wysocki,Wanli Ma,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BendTwin，一个弯曲感知的可微分弹簧-质点框架，用于从视频中重建可变形物体并预测其未来状态。它通过引入弯曲刚度和阻尼来增强机械稳定性，性能优于仅使用轴向弹簧的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于弹簧-质点的物理驱动重建方法通常只依赖轴向弹簧，这种简化过于粗糙，当物理模型粗糙化时会导致机械约束不足，难以保持稳定的局部形变。这限制了其在从视频重建物体并预测其动力学时的保真度和稳定性。

Method: 提出BendTwin框架。核心创新是：在局部表面三元组（而非仅边）上引入弯曲刚度和阻尼。这通过惩罚偏离静止角度的偏差来实现，从而对高阶形变进行正则化。这种方法在保持弹簧-质点系统简单性的同时，增强了其机械稳定性。

Result: 实验证明，BendTwin在性能上持续优于仅使用轴向弹簧的PhysTwin基线方法。消融研究进一步表明，弯曲约束在不同降采样率下均能保持系统稳定性，并始终改进了原始PhysTwin的性能。

Conclusion: BendTwin提供了一种有效的方法，能够从稀疏的RGB-D视频中构建出机械行为上更真实的数字孪生体，为机器人规划和交互中的物理一致性动态预测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reconstructing objects with mechanical properties from video observations enables physically consistent dynamic prediction, benefiting robotics planning and interaction. Existing spring--mass based physical driven reconstruction approaches offer efficient and differentiable physical reconstruction, but they typically rely on axial springs alone. Such formulations oversimplify the underlying structural mechanics and can become mechanically under-constrained when the physical graph is coarsened, limiting their ability to preserve stable local deformation. We present BendTwin, a bending-aware differentiable spring--mass framework for video-based reconstruction and future prediction of deformable objects. BendTwin introduces bending stiffness and damping over local surface triplets, penalizing deviations from rest angles and regularizing higher-order deformation. These bending constraints improve mechanical stability while preserving the simplicity of spring--mass system. Experiments show that BendTwin consistently outperforms the axial-only PhysTwin baseline. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the bending constraints maintain system stability across different downsampling ratios and consistently improve upon the original PhysTwin formulation. Overall, BendTwin provides an effective approach for constructing mechanically faithful digital twins from sparse-view RGB-D videos.

</details>


### [96] [Support Operation Factorization: Compositional Readout of Frozen Vision Encoders under Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06174)
*Zhongyao Wang,Wanli Ouyang,Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的评估协议和读出方法，以更准确地评估冻结视觉编码器的组合性能，解决了现有方法中"操作洗钱"的缺陷，并在实验中验证了其有效性，同时也揭示了学习分配的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准因子探针在评估视觉编码器的组合性能时存在"操作洗钱"问题，即奖励了重复使用相同预测槽位的多个操作，导致评估结果不准确。需要一种新方法，能够分离并独立评估两个关键问题：在已知操作网格的情况下，载体是否能够组合保留的绑定；以及该操作网格是否能够从扁平的单元标签中恢复出来。

Method: 提出了一种"注入对齐的留一单元出"评估协议，并引入了SO-OPF读出机制。该方法将单元能量分解为两个独立部分：支持显著性（表示输入的支持程度）和竞争操作后验（表示操作分配的后验概率）。通过这种因式分解，可以分别评估上述两个关键问题，避免聚合分数导致的混淆。

Result: 在冻结的DINOv3视觉特征上进行实验：1) 在已知因子分配的情况下，方法在Shapes3D-Extended数据集上达到0.874的注入准确率，在全局图像不相交的COCO数据集上达到0.799；2) 从扁平标签学习分配时，准确率分别达到0.769和0.762。在Shapes3D数据集上，使用匹配轴感知监督时，因式载体将学习分配准确率从0.653提升至0.841（相比密集载体），并消除了其"洗钱"差距。SigLIP2编码器在COCO上也复制了这种分离效果。然而，在新构建的MuJoCo子数据集上，学习分配的准确率较低（DINOv3为0.569，SigLIP2为0.484），并出现了显著的槽位坍塌现象。

Conclusion: 因式读出机制和注入评估协议能够有效评估视觉编码器在已知操作网格下的组合性能，并在两个基底（DINOv3和SigLIP2）上成功恢复保留的绑定。该方法同时暴露而非掩盖了学习分配的一个特定失败边界，即渲染器特性相关的性能下降。然而，研究结果也表明，仅从扁平标签进行普遍且可靠的操作网格恢复目前仍无法实现，这指出了该领域的一个根本性挑战。

Abstract: Compositional analysis of frozen vision encoders should determine both what changed and where it changed. Standard factor probes score these axes separately, however, and can reward multiple operations that reuse the same predicted slot. We call this failure operation laundering. We introduce an injectively aligned leave-one-cell-out protocol over support x operation grids and SO-OPF, a readout that factors cell energy into support salience and a competitive operation posterior. This formulation separates two questions that aggregate scores conflate: whether the carrier composes held-out bindings when the grid is known, and whether that grid can be recovered from flat cell labels. With frozen DINOv3 features, known factorial assignment reaches 0.874 injective accuracy on Shapes3D-Extended and 0.799 on globally image-disjoint COCO; learning the assignment from flat labels reaches 0.769 and 0.762, respectively. Under matched-axis-aware supervision on Shapes3D, the factored carrier improves learned-assignment accuracy from 0.653 to 0.841 over a dense carrier and eliminates its laundering gap. SigLIP2 replicates the COCO separation. A rebuilt MuJoCo substrate exposes a boundary: learned-assignment accuracy is 0.569 with DINOv3 and 0.484 with SigLIP2, with substantial slot collapse. Thus factored readout and injective evaluation recover held-out bindings on two substrates while exposing, rather than hiding, a renderer-specific failure boundary; they do not establish universal recovery from flat labels.

</details>


### [97] [EvReflection: Event-Driven Micro-Dynamics for Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06184)
*Jiaxiao Wang,Dachun Kai,Huyue Zhu,Quanquan Hu,Zhenyang Xu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用事件相机信号解决图像反射去除问题，通过捕捉反射层与透射层的差异运动来分离层，其方法EvReflection在合成和真实数据上均显著优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前反射去除方法主要依赖单帧静态图像先验，由于反射层与透射层固有的歧义性，导致分离后存在严重的残余伪影。

Method: 提出事件驱动的反射去除网络EvReflection，包含微动态解耦器和视差注意力校正器。设计视差感知模拟流程，并构建首个真实世界数据集EVR²。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中均达到最先进性能，PSNR分别超过次优方法1.6 dB和1.2 dB。

Conclusion: 利用事件相机的动态线索可有效打破反射去除的歧义性，显著提升分离效果。贡献包括新的网络架构、首个真实世界数据集及公开的代码与模型。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in reflection removal, current methods primarily exploit static image priors from a single frame and still suffer from severe residual artifacts due to the inherent ambiguity between the reflection and transmission layers. In this paper, we propose leveraging event signals to break this ambiguity. By employing event cameras to capture micro-dynamics, we reveal the differential motion between these two layers. We thereby present a novel event-driven reflection removal network, EvReflection, that utilizes these dynamic cues for layer separation. Specifically, we design a Micro-Dynamics Decoupler to disentangle layer-specific motions from event streams as priors, which then guide a Parallax-Attention Rectifier to cleanly remove artifacts from the RGB image. Furthermore, to address data scarcity, we develop a parallax-aware simulation pipeline and construct the EVR$^2$ benchmark dataset, the first real-world dataset for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvReflection achieves state-of-the-art performance on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, surpassing the best competing method by more than 1.6 dB and 1.2 dB in PSNR, respectively. The code, dataset, and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/JiaxiaoWang/EvReflection.

</details>


### [98] [HOPE: Hand-Object Pressure Estimation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06192)
*Subin Jeon,Byungjun Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOPE框架通过将压力估计重新定义为手心视频预测问题，利用单目视频输入预测手部网格上的压力和接触情况，并在多种设置下实现了泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的视觉压力估计方法主要局限于平面表面和单一图像输入，难以应用于多样化的手-物体动态交互，因此需要一种能够处理复杂、动态交互场景的新方法。

Method: HOPE框架包含两个关键组件：1）将触觉手套压力、平面传感器压力和距离基手-物体接触标注提升到共享的手部顶点空间，以规范化压力学习；2）引入顶点锚定的视频转换器，将每个顶点视为持久令牌，聚合时序视觉特征和手部姿势，并使用接触门控压力头预测压力。

Result: 在OpenTouch、PressureVisionDB和手-物体接触基准上的实验验证了HOPE在物体压力、表面压力和接触监督的HOI设置中的有效性。HOPE能够泛化到裸手自我中心和野外视频，产生超越仅接触或平面压力基线的联合接触和压力预测。

Conclusion: HOPE通过将压力估计重新定义为手心视频预测问题并引入顶点锚定的视频转换器，实现了对动态手-物体交互中压力估计的有效泛化，为接触丰富的手-物体交互理解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Estimating physical pressure from vision is essential for understanding contact-rich hand-object interaction. However, prior vision-based pressure estimation methods are largely limited to planar surfaces and single image input, making them difficult to apply to dynamic hand-object interaction with diverse objects. We instead formulate pressure estimation as a hand-centric video prediction problem with monocular video as input. This formulation predicts temporally evolving per-vertex normal pressure and contact directly on the hand mesh, yielding a unified output space independent of object shape and sensor layout. Building on this formulation, we propose \textbf{HOPE}, a framework with two key components. First, we lift tactile-glove pressure, planar-sensor pressure, and distance-based hand-object contact annotations into a shared hand vertex space, allowing bare-hand contact data to regularize pressure learning where metric labels are unavailable. Second, we introduce a vertex-anchored video transformer that treats each vertex as a persistent token, aggregates visual features and hand pose over time, and uses a contact-gated pressure head to enforce that pressure vanishes without contact. Experiments on OpenTouch, PressureVisionDB, and hand-object contact benchmarks validate HOPE across object-pressure, surface-pressure, and contact-supervised HOI settings. Despite using metric pressure supervision primarily from gloved-hand videos, HOPE generalizes to bare-hand egocentric and in-the-wild videos, producing joint contact and pressure predictions beyond the scope of contact-only or planar-pressure baselines.

</details>


### [99] [CFGPNet: Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network Framework for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06205)
*Nima Hatami,Karim Faez,Saeed Sharifian,Hamidreza Amindavar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CFGPNet，一个用于多光谱目标检测的跨注意力融合梯度编程网络，通过改进的骨干网络、跨模态注意力模块、选择性融合网络和梯度辅助分支，有效解决了跨模态交互不足、融合不稳定和计算成本高的问题，在多个公开数据集上取得了优异的性能与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-T（可见光-红外）目标检测方法存在跨模态交互不充分、因模态分布差异导致的融合不稳定，以及基于重型注意力机制的架构计算成本高等问题，限制了其在复杂场景下的鲁棒性和实用性。本文旨在提出一个更有效、高效的多模态融合检测框架。

Method: CFGPNet框架包含多个技术创新：1）使用改进的GELAN骨干网络（集成RepViT风格的重参数化模块）以增强特征表征并保持计算效率；2）引入CrossCEA模块，用于增强跨模态特征交互并减少分支间的冗余信息传递；3）设计ASAF网络，通过密集特征聚合和基于选择性注意力的强调，生成紧凑且判别性的融合表征；4）在每个CFGPNet变体中集成可编程梯度辅助分支，以改善梯度传播和优化质量。

Result: 在FLIR、M3FD、LLVIP、VEDAI和MFAD五个公开多光谱基准数据集上的实验表明，CFGPNet在不同场景、目标尺度和模态平衡条件下均表现出强大且一致的性能。具体地，在FLIR数据集上达到80.7% mAP50 / 45.0% mAP50:95，M3FD数据集上为89.9% / 63.4%，LLVIP数据集上为97.8% / 68.9%，VEDAI数据集上为83.3% / 56.9%，MFAD数据集上为83.4% / 61.8%。结果证明该框架在三个模型规模上均能提供出色的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: CFGPNet是一个有效且实用的解决方案，通过其创新的跨注意力融合机制和梯度优化设计，成功解决了多模态检测中的关键挑战，实现了在多个基准数据集上的先进性能，并展现了良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: RGB--T object detection exploits the complementary strengths of visible and infrared imagery, supporting robust perception in low-light, adverse-weather, and complex multi-scale environments. However, existing methods still suffer from insufficient cross-modal interaction, unstable fusion from modality distribution gaps, and the high computational cost of heavy attention-based architectures. To address these issues, CFGPNet is proposed, a Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network framework for multispectral object detection. CFGPNet uses an improved GELAN backbone with RepViT-style re-parameterized blocks to strengthen feature representation while preserving computational efficiency. A Cross Computation Efficient Attention (CrossCEA) module is introduced to enhance cross-modal feature interaction and reduce redundant information transfer between visible and thermal branches. To generate compact and discriminative fused representations, an Attention Selection and Aggregation Fusion (ASAF) network combines dense feature aggregation with selective attention-based emphasis. Moreover, a programmable-gradient auxiliary branch is integrated into each CFGPNet variant to improve gradient delivery and optimization quality. Experiments on five public multispectral benchmarks, FLIR, M3FD, LLVIP, VEDAI, and MFAD, demonstrate that CFGPNet achieves strong and consistent performance across diverse scenes, object scales, and modality balances. In particular, the framework attains 80.7% mAP50 / 45.0% mAP50:95 on FLIR, 89.9% / 63.4% on M3FD, and 97.8% / 68.9% on LLVIP. It also reaches 83.3% / 56.9% on VEDAI and 83.4% / 61.8% on MFAD. These results show that CFGPNet is an effective, practical solution offering useful accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across three model scales. The code, data, and fine-tuned models are available at https://github.com/NimaHatami99/CFGPNet.

</details>


### [100] [EmoWorld: A Decoupled Affective Field for Controllable Emotional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06231)
*Bingyuan Wang,Baistan Zhyldyzbekov,Kunyu Feng,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoWorld是一个解耦视频情感生成框架，通过将情感氛围、语义线索和时间进程分别处理，提升了文本到视频和图像到视频生成中的情感表达精确性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器将全局情感氛围、承载情感的语义线索和时间进程纠缠在单一文本条件中，限制了情感表达的精确性和可控性。本文旨在解耦这些因素，以实现更精细的情感控制。

Method: EmoWorld框架在一个冻结的流匹配视频扩散Transformer（Video DiT）中解耦情感因素。一次性准备阶段从几何保持的中性和情感编辑全景图中提取层特定的情感方向和可复用的线索库。推理时，通过视觉氛围引导（VAS）将氛围方向注入隐藏状态，通过语义情感引导（SAS）隔离出单独可扩展的提示残差用于语义线索，并通过时间情感引导（TAS）在去噪和视频时间内插端点残差场。

Result: 在Wan2.2模型上，VAS将目标情感对齐度提升了19%，同时将时间波动指标降低了48%；SAS将目标情感对齐度提升了37%，并增加了36%的可检测情感线索；TAS将过渡单调性提升了15%。该框架在27种情感类别、文本到视频和图像到视频设置中均表现出有效性，并证明其在多个Video-DiT主干网络间的可移植性。

Conclusion: EmoWorld提供了一种有效的解耦方法，显著提升了视频生成中的情感表达和控制能力。该框架无需更新生成器参数，支持相机条件合成，具有良好的通用性和可移植性。

Abstract: Emotion shapes how viewers interpret a scene, yet existing video generators entangle global atmosphere, affect-bearing semantic cues, and temporal progression within a single text condition. We present EmoWorld, a framework that decouples these factors within a frozen flow-matching video diffusion transformer (Video DiT). A one-time preparation stage extracts layer-specific affect directions and a reusable cue library from geometry-preserving neutral and emotion-edited panoramas. At inference, Visual Atmosphere Steering (VAS) injects atmosphere directions into hidden states, Semantic Affective Steering (SAS) isolates a separately scalable prompt residual for semantic cues, and Temporal Affective Steering (TAS) interpolates endpoint residual fields across denoising and video time. On Wan2.2, VAS improves target-emotion alignment by 19% while reducing a temporal-fluctuation proxy by 48%; SAS improves target-emotion alignment by 37% and increases detected affect-bearing cues by 36%; and TAS improves transition monotonicity by 15% over the strongest baseline. EmoWorld is evaluated across 27 emotion categories in text-to-video and image-to-video settings, demonstrates portability across multiple Video-DiT backbones, and supports camera-conditioned composition without updating generator parameters.

</details>


### [101] [Depth-Guided Video Object Counting in Crowded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06236)
*Yuanjing Xu,Xinyan Liu,Weidong Chen,Zixuan Zou,Linhao Zhang,Zhuangzhe Meng,Antoni B. Chan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度引导的检测器（DG-Det）用于拥挤场景中的视频目标计数，通过融合深度信息显著提升了在拥挤遮挡条件下的计数准确性，并发布了配套的RGB-D数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标计数方法主要依赖RGB图像信息，在拥挤、存在遮挡的场景中辨别能力受限，导致计数性能下降。为了提升模型在复杂场景下的鲁棒性和准确性，亟需探索更有效的信息融合与表征方法。

Method: 1. 提出深度引导检测器（DG-Det），通过多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力机制融合深度信息与RGB特征，增强空间理解。
2. 引入显式遮挡预测模块，显式地处理物体间的遮挡关系。
3. 设计统一的跨帧去重后处理框架，消除视频中因物体移动或检测冗余导致的重复计数。
4. 构建并发布了一个包含深度信息、支持多类别计数的RGB-D视频目标计数新数据集。

Result: 在提出的新数据集上进行的大量实验表明，所提方法相比现有基线方法，平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了62.01%，均方根误差（RMSE）也得到了一致性提升，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过深度融合RGB与深度信息，并结合针对性的遮挡处理与去重策略，有效解决了拥挤场景下的视频目标计数难题。所提出的方法和发布的新数据集为后续相关研究提供了有力的基线与数据资源。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [102] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一种无GAN的流匹配框架，用于非配对图像到图像翻译。它用一个学习的逐特征门控取代了传统的全局控制，该门控根据源特征与目标特征分布的距离来决定保留或修改哪些特征，从而在生成逼真图像的同时更好地保留原始结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的非配对图像翻译方法通常使用单一的全局噪声或指导值来控制图像中内容的保留，这无法有效区分需要保留的内容和需要改变的外观，导致翻译结果的结构保留不佳或不真实。

Method: PRISM采用流匹配框架，其核心是一个学习的逐特征门控。该门控的空间先验由源特征相对于目标特征分布的标准化距离决定，距离远的特征被释放（改变），距离近的特征被保留。同一个门控同时控制两个关键过程：1）初始化：将真实源潜在表示与任务匹配的损坏（如用于结构保持翻译的AdaIN或用于结构变化翻译的部分锚定）混合；2）常微分方程积分过程中的传输时序。此外，该门控支持在推理时通过文本或检测器进行局部覆盖，无需重新训练。

Result: 在五个自然和生物医学基准（AFHQ猫->狗、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、日->夜重光照、虚拟染色、乳腺冷冻->永久组织病理学）上的评估表明，在相同的划分协议下，PRISM在四个基准上取得了最佳的Inception FID和KID分数，在第五个基准上也取得了有竞争力的结果。在组织病理学任务上，PRISM生成的核计数比率最接近理想值。

Conclusion: PRISM通过其创新的逐特征门控机制，有效地解决了非配对图像翻译中全局控制导致的内容保留与外观改变之间的权衡问题。实验结果表明，PRISM在多个基准测试中优于现有方法，在生成逼真结果的同时，实现了更优的结构保留能力，为非配对图像翻译提供了一个强有力的解决方案。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [103] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对孟加拉手语识别，提出了一个专家验证的新数据集RSBdSL38和一个轻量级注意力卷积网络，该模型在保证高准确率的同时，大幅减少参数和计算量，适合在智能手机等移动设备上实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的孟加拉手语识别系统主要依赖于未经专家验证的受控环境数据集，并且采用参数量大、计算成本高的预训练骨干网络，这些模型不适合在个人设备上进行高效、实时的推理，从而限制了手语识别技术的普及和应用。

Method: 研究包含两个核心部分：1. 构建了一个名为RSBdSL38的新数据集，包含10,874张来自真实手语使用者的专家验证图像，覆盖了孟加拉语字母表中全部51个字母所对应的38种手部手势。2. 提出了一种从零开始训练的轻量级注意力卷积网络架构，该架构集成了分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力机制、多尺度深度可分手部特征提取模块、双池化和Swish激活函数。

Result: 模型在RSBdSL38数据集上从零训练达到96.37%的准确率，性能接近使用ImageNet预训练的更重模型。在六个公开的孟加拉手语基准测试中，重新训练后的准确率在92.95%到98.33%之间。该模型参数量（约29.8万）和计算量（MACs）比预训练基准模型少一个数量级以上。量化后模型体积仅为0.48 MB，在普通智能手机上的单张图像推理时间约为3.98毫秒。

Conclusion: 本研究成功地将基准测试上的高准确率转化为可在资源受限设备上实际部署的可访问解决方案。发布的RSBdSL38数据集和轻量级模型为孟加拉手语识别社区提供了重要的基础资源，显著降低了部署成本，推动了手语识别技术的实用化和普及。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [104] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS（多人权威共享状态）模型，通过借鉴多人游戏架构，将世界动态与视角渲染解耦，以权威共享状态作为唯一递归记忆和同步参考，从而解决现有视频世界模型在多人环境中计算冗余、视角不一致和扩展性差的问题，并在大规模多人模拟中实现了优越的状态准确性和低跨视角不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频世界模型在多人环境中存在根本缺陷：它们将世界状态与视角相关的视觉特征纠缠在一起，导致三个主要问题：计算冗余、视角不一致性以及可扩展性差。这些限制阻碍了其在多智能体环境中的有效应用。

Method: 受多人游戏架构启发，MAS模型明确地将世界动态与视角渲染解耦。它包含两个学习组件：1) 逻辑引擎：基于联合动作推进一个全局、权威的类型化状态，不依赖任何手写的状态转换函数，并作为模型唯一的递归记忆和同步参考。2) 渲染引擎：基于该共享状态，按需为任意请求的相机生成独立且一致的视图。

Result: 在匹配的多人Snake基准测试上，与现有的多视角基线模型相比，MAS实现了优越的状态准确性和更低的跨视角不一致性。其关键能力在于能够推进具有1,024个并发玩家、持续10,000个递归步骤的预测世界，展示了其卓越的扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文的研究表明，显式的、权威的状态建模为构建可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了一个切实可行的基础。MAS模型通过解耦状态逻辑和视觉渲染，有效解决了多人环境中的关键挑战，为未来的大规模动态环境模拟开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [105] [OTLesMix: Wasserstein Barycenter and Optimal Transport Map for Synthetic Lesion Generation with Diverse Shapes and Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06264)
*Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OTLesMix的新型医学图像合成方法，利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输技术生成多样化的训练样本，以提升脑部病变分割模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混合的数据增强方法在生成训练样本时，存在病灶形状和位置变化有限的不足，限制了模型的泛化能力。为了提升分割模型对多样化病理特征的识别能力，需要更先进、能生成更真实多样病变的合成技术。

Method: 引入OTLesMix方法，该方法基于Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划，通过最优传输映射将真实样本混合，从而合成兼具真实性和多样性的新图像，特别是能生成更多样的病变形状和位置。

Result: 在三个脑部病变分割任务上的评估表明，与未使用合成数据训练的模型相比，OTLesMix将Dice分数提高了2.9至6.6个点，并且其性能优于当前最先进的基于混合的数据增强方法。

Conclusion: OTLesMix是一种有效的医学图像合成方法，能够生成高质量、多样化的训练样本，显著提升脑部病变分割模型的性能，优于现有混合增强策略。

Abstract: The development of deep learning over the past decade has revolutionized medical imaging segmentation, allowing the extraction of precise descriptors from large volumes to characterize pathologies. Data augmentation is a technique widely regarded as a way to improve model training. It includes simple transformations like spatial operations or intensity modifications, but also more advanced synthesis techniques. Their goal is to generate new realistic samples from an existing dataset to diversify the images used during training. Among them, several propose different mixing strategies to combine real samples. However, one of their major shortcomings is to yield limited variability in terms of generated lesion shapes and locations. In this work, we introduce a novel image synthesis method, called OTLesMix, that leverages Wasserstein barycenter and optimal transport plan to generate realistic and diverse samples. We evaluated our method on three brain lesion segmentation tasks, on which it improves the Dice score compared to a model trained without synthetic data by 2.9 to 6.6 points, and outperforms state-of-the-art mix-based methods.

</details>


### [106] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种基于智能手机照片的牙齿定位和编号模型，使用定制的Mask R-CNN管道，以实现低成本、可扩展的远程口腔健康筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔健康问题影响全球数十亿人，但专业牙科护理的成本和可及性限制了预防性口腔保健。现有研究依赖于临床级放射线照片或口内相机图像，这些对于公众自我筛查来说不可用。

Method: 开发了一个定制的Mask R-CNN管道，在1,272张带有注释的智能手机图像上进行训练。该管道包括两个领域知情机制：用于减轻人工色偏的掩膜灰度世界白平衡算法，以及用于强制结构有效性和抑制误报的解剖学约束检测层。评估包括四个阶段：内部保留测试、独立外部测试、描述性消融研究和基于折叠的训练稳定性分析。

Result: 在内部测试集上，模型实现了实例掩膜AP@50为0.818，类别感知PQ为0.780，操作F1为0.884。训练稳定性显示模型间差异有限：在十次运行中，实例掩膜AP@50的标准差为0.009。在外部数据集上，模型实现了实例掩膜AP@50为0.901，类别感知PQ为0.832，操作F1为0.928，尽管存在人群、传感器和采集协议的差异。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，消费级智能手机图像可以支持自动化的牙齿级解剖图谱绘制，为资源受限环境下的远程筛查和远程牙科提供了可扩展、潜在低成本的基础。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [107] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc通过为基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归引入不确定性量化，提高了6-DoF定位的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归方法产生确定性预测，丢弃了可能改善系统鲁棒性和下游决策的aleatoric（数据内在）不确定性。

Method: UQ-Loc扩展了LightLoc架构，新增一个各向异性高斯协方差头，为每个体素预测完整的3x3正定协方差矩阵。训练采用负对数似然损失并辅以基于k近邻的空间平滑正则化器。推理时使用修改的SC2-PCR求解器，结合不确定性加权的种子评分和马氏距离内点测试。采用期望校准误差作为评估预测不确定性质量的原则性指标。

Result: 实验表明，UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上实现了一致改进，同时生成的协方差具有良好的校准性。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性量化集成到基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归中，UQ-Loc在提升定位精度的同时，提供了可用于下游决策的可靠不确定性估计。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [108] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究评估超分辨率重建对白质高信号分割的影响，发现主要风险是擦除小病变而非产生幻觉，且自监督模型ECLARE表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床FLAIR扫描常采用厚层采集，导致通过平面分辨率低。超分辨率技术可用于恢复各向同性体积，但尚不清楚在白质高信号分割前应用超分辨率是否会保留病变内容：模型可能擦除真实的小病变或产生虚假病变。

Method: 使用ADNI队列中29名个体的1毫米各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描（专家手动分割WMH），将其降级为模拟的3毫米和5毫米厚层切片。应用三种重建方法（多对比度隐式神经表示INR、单对比度自监督模型ECLARE、立方插值）上采样至高分辨率网格。使用四种WMH分割方法，并在最敏感于小病变的MARS-WMH下进行评估，指标包括检测敏感度、擦除率和幻觉率。

Result: 超分辨率的主要效应是擦除小的真实病变，而非产生幻觉，且随层厚增加而加剧，但所有重建方法仍优于原始厚层切片的病变检测。在两种层厚下，ECLARE在恢复小病变信号方面表现最佳，而INR的效果与立方插值相当。

Conclusion: 在白质高信号分割前应用超分辨率重建存在擦除小病变的风险，但自监督模型ECLARE能更有效地恢复小病变信号，有助于提高厚层临床扫描的病变检测能力。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从人类视频学习机器人操作技能的新框架，通过提取与具体实体无关的可操作性affordance，解决了人机之间的embodiment不匹配问题，并实现了零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频中学习机器人操作技能具有可扩展性前景，但由于人与机器人之间的embodiment不匹配，这一过程面临挑战。一个有前景的解决方案是学习与embodiment无关的、以物体为中心的可操作性affordance。

Method: 本文提出了一个框架，该框架利用自我中心视角的人类视频，并结合先进的3D运动结构恢复和手部网格重建技术，来提取可操作的affordance，包括指明在哪里交互的视觉affordance、如何抓取的抓取affordance以及如何移动的轨迹affordance。在此基础上，研究者构建了大规模数据集EgoAffordance，并引入了一个基于大型视觉-语言模型的统一基础模型VLAff，该模型学习所有可操作affordance之间的跨模态关联。

Result: 本文构建了一个包含20.4万集（episode）、560万个视觉affordance以及1160万个抓取和轨迹affordance的大规模数据集EgoAffordance。实验表明，VLAff不仅在视觉affordance预测上达到了最先进的性能，还能有效应用于实际的机器人任务，如零样本操作和affordance引导的机器人学习。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架通过学习与embodiment无关的可操作性affordance，成功架起了从人类视频到机器人技能学习的桥梁。VLAff模型展示了其作为统一基础模型，在理解和生成多种affordance以指导机器人操作方面的有效性和先进性。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [110] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合结构传感器与AI分析的新型实时监测与碰撞检测方法，旨在提升干线铁路运营安全并支持自动驾驶转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干线铁路因严苛的安全要求和复杂环境，全自动运行（GoA4）应用受限。现有基于摄像头、雷达和激光雷达的感知系统能检测物体，但在可靠识别碰撞、碾压等事件方面能力有限。

Method: 提出了一种整合结构传感技术与AI数据分析的实时车辆状态监测与碰撞检测框架。

Result: 该框架实现了三项关键应用：(1) 自动检测碰撞、结构损伤和碾压事件；(2) 支持基于状态的持续监测维护；(3) 提供长期数据分析以优化车辆设计。

Conclusion: 该方法的可行性得到验证，展示了其在增强运营安全、实现预测性维护以及支持干线铁路向全自动驾驶过渡方面的潜力。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>


### [111] [Failing Gracefully: Mitigating Impact of Inevitable Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05313)
*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的安全评估框架，用于量化家庭服务机器人在发生故障时对周围环境（人、宠物、物品）产生影响的概率和严重性，并开发了配套的仿真平台FailBench，旨在支持开发更安全可靠的机器人规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境复杂多变，服务机器人在此类环境中运行时，极易因软件、硬件故障或不可预测的交互而失效。尽管研究人员致力于最小化故障，但一些故障仍不可避免。因此，为了确保机器人在现实世界中安全部署，必须有一种方法能够评估和减轻故障带来的潜在后果，这是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 论文提出了两项主要贡献：1) 一种新颖的安全形式化评估方法，它综合评估故障期间机器人与环境中不同实体发生有影响交互的**概率**以及交互后果的**严重性**，通过量化故障对不同实体的影响来指导规划决策。2) 开发了名为**FailBench**的、基于MuJoCo的仿真测试平台，用于系统性地研究机器人在多种故障模式（如感知问题、执行器故障）下与环境的交互。

Result: 本文提出的安全形式化方法使机器人能够在规划过程中，**在任务效率与安全之间做出权衡与知情决策**。FailBench平台则为评估和改进机器人在故障情况下的行为提供了一个标准化的、可重复的研究基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出的安全形式化框架和FailBench仿真平台，共同为开发更安全、更稳健的**运动规划算法**和**学习型策略**奠定了理论基础和评估工具，从而推动服务机器人在真实家庭环境中的可靠应用。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>


### [112] [Unified Planning-Learning Framework for Robust UUV Navigation Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05365)
*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.

</details>


### [113] [World-to-Wrist: Task-Conditioned Future Wrist Modeling for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05369)
*Yuhao Pan,Haosong Peng,Zhengshen Zhang,Zhengyang Yan,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Chujie Wang,Tianyu Qi,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了W2-VLA模型，通过引入任务条件化的未来手腕建模，改进了视觉-语言-动作模型在精细机器人操作中的表现。该模型将手腕视角视为受全局任务背景影响的独特信息源，并设计了W2-CoT数据合成管道提供辅助监督，在多个基准测试中实现了更好的精细操作性能和超过80Hz的动作生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型通常将主视角和手腕视角观测作为并行的视觉输入处理，忽略了它们在机器人操作中的不同角色。然而，精细操作需要基于全局任务背景来预测手腕局部交互将如何演变。这种忽略手腕视角独特作用的方法限制了模型在精细和接触敏感操作中的性能。

Method: 本文提出W2-VLA模型，采用任务条件化的未来手腕建模方法。该方法使用一组潜在建模令牌作为视觉语言模型和手腕预测器之间的紧凑接口，基于该接口和观察到的手腕历史记录，手腕预测器预测未来手腕潜在状态，并将其转化为未来感知的上下文用于动作预测。此外，还引入了W2-CoT合成管道，生成描述操作进度、物理转换线索和手腕局部证据的结构化注释，为任务条件化的潜在接口提供辅助监督。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0基准测试和实际机器人操作任务上的实验表明，该方法在单臂和双臂设置下均提升了精细操作和接触敏感操作的性能，同时保持超过80Hz的动作生成速率。任务条件化的未来手腕建模有效改善了操作性能，W2-CoT注释提供了有效的辅助监督。

Conclusion: W2-VLA通过任务条件化的未来手腕建模，成功解决了现有VLA模型对主视角和手腕视角观测处理不当的问题。该方法通过将手腕视角视为受全局任务影响的独特信息源，并结合结构化辅助监督，显著提升了精细操作性能，为机器人操作任务提供了更有效的视觉-语言-动作建模方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often treat main-view and wrist-view observations as parallel visual inputs, overlooking their distinct roles in robot manipulation. Fine-grained manipulation, however, benefits from anticipating how wrist-local interactions may evolve under the global task context. To address this limitation, we present World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA), a VLA model for fine-grained robot manipulation with task-conditioned future wrist modeling. Given current multi-view observations and a task instruction, W2-VLA contextualizes a set of latent modeling tokens as a compact interface between the vision-language model and the wrist predictor. Conditioned on this interface and the observed wrist history, the predictor forecasts future wrist latents, which are transformed into future-aware context for action prediction. In addition, we introduce W2-CoT, a synthesis pipeline that produces structured annotations describing manipulation progress, physical transition cues, and wrist-local evidence. These annotations provide auxiliary supervision that shapes the task-conditioned latent interface. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved fine-grained and contact-sensitive manipulation across both single-arm and bimanual settings, while maintaining action-generation rates above 80 Hz.

</details>


### [114] [Sliding Sensors: Configurable Confidence in State Estimation for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05410)
*Ella Walsh,Spencer Teetaert,Eric Diller,Timothy D. Barfoot,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Continuum robots often operate in uncertain environments, where accurate state estimation is essential for safe interactions. Estimate uncertainty is inherently spatially non-uniform: confidence varies depending on where measurements are available. Global estimation accuracy is not always the top priority, but rather achieving sufficient confidence at task-relevant locations along the robot. This extended abstract introduces mechanically reconfigurable sensing enabling uncertainty-shaping in state estimation for continuum robots. We present a concept hardware design demonstrating the feasibility of longitudinal translation of a sensor within a continuum robot. We demonstrate that state estimation confidence can be reconfigured by varying the sensor location, and show a reduction of full-body shape estimation errors when sliding the sensor back and forth over time, compared to a single fixed tip sensor.

</details>


### [115] [ARGUS: Aligning Robot Scene Geometry Under Shifting Views with Large 3D Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05579)
*Rishik Sathua,Haonan Chen,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARGUS，一种利用大规模3D视觉模型将任意相机视角的图像观察对齐到规范视角的观察预处理流程，以解决机器人操作策略中场景几何与视角纠缠的问题，从而提升策略从视角多样数据中学习和泛化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉运动策略常将场景几何与特定视角纠缠，导致策略学习的是图像中物体的位置而非任务空间中的位置，这限制了策略从视角多样数据集（如DROID、BridgeV2）中学习的能力，并影响其泛化到训练数据未覆盖视角的性能。

Method: 提出ARGUS观察预处理流程：利用大规模3D视觉模型，将来自任意相机视角的输入图像观察对齐到一个统一的规范视角，随后再将其输入下游的视觉运动策略。

Result: 在多种训练数据集（从固定多视角配置到高度变化的相机布置）上的实验表明，ARGUS在有限视角和多样视角训练场景下均优于先前方法。该方法能够利用视角多样数据进行学习，并将收敛到高成功率的速度提升4-6倍，展现了其通过简化观察空间来提升学习效率的能力。

Conclusion: 利用大规模3D视觉模型进行视角对齐，能有效降低视觉运动策略的学习负担，使其能更高效地从大规模、视角多样的机器人数据集中学习，并提升其泛化性能。

Abstract: Large-scale visuomotor policies have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of robot manipulation tasks. However, despite this success, manipulation polices often entangle scene geometry with the corresponding viewpoint, learning where objects lie in an image rather than where it lies in the task space. This entanglement inherently limits the corresponding policy's ability to learn from viewpoint-diverse datasets (ex. DROID, BridgeV2) and generalize beyond the viewpoints captured in their training data. In this work, we present ARGUS, an observation pre-processing pipeline that uses large-scale 3D vision models to align image observations from arbitrary camera viewpoints into a canonical viewpoint before passing it to downstream visuomotor policies. Experiments across training datasets with varying levels of viewpoint diversity, from fixed multi-view camera configurations to highly varied camera placements, show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches across both limited-view and view-diverse training regimes. In efficiency comparisons, ARGUS demonstrates an ability to learn from view-diverse data, converging to high success rates 4-6x faster than previous methods by leveraging a simplified observation space. Overall, our findings show that leveraging large-scale 3D vision models reduces the learning burden on visuomotor policies, enabling more efficient learning from large-scale, viewpoint-diverse robot datasets.

</details>


### [116] [PathCover: A Fast Convex Decomposition along a Path via Randomized Iterative Space Partitioning (RISP) on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05586)
*Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Guoquan Huang,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了PathCover框架及核心算法RISP，通过一种新颖的随机算法，能直接从原始点云数据中、在预期的线性时间内生成无障碍的凸多面体序列，从而为自主机器人的轨迹规划提供安全的运动走廊，其计算速度比现有最优方法快一个数量级，并已在四旋翼和四足机器人上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航需要快速生成无障碍区域以进行轨迹规划。然而，现有的运动走廊生成器难以满足实时、传感器速率计算的瓶颈限制。

Method: 引入基于RISP（一种新颖的随机算法）的PathCover框架。RISP算法在温和的消除条件下，直接从原始点云数据中以预期的线性时间构建凸多面体。该框架生成一系列重叠的、无障碍的多面体序列，以安全地约束后续的模型预测控制（MPC）和轨迹优化。算法在数学上保证了有限步终止，并确保沿任何无障碍参考路径的连续进展。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界LiDAR数据集上的大量基准测试表明，该方法比现有最优方法的速度提升了一个数量级，同时保持了相当的走廊体积。完整的流程通过高保真四旋翼仿真以及物理部署在四足机器人上（使用实时LiDAR感知在受限环境中导航）得到了验证。

Conclusion: PathCover框架通过其核心的RISP随机算法，成功解决了自主机器人导航中实时生成无障碍运动走廊的计算瓶颈问题，实现了显著的速度提升，并经过仿真与物理机器人实验验证了其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation requires the rapid generation of obstacle-free regions for trajectory planning. However, existing corridor generators struggle to meet real-time, sensor-rate computational constraints. To resolve this bottleneck, we introduce PathCover, a framework driven by RISP; a novel randomized algorithm that constructs convex polytopes directly from raw point cloud data in expected linear time under a mild probabilistic elimination condition. PathCover generates sequences of overlapping, obstacle-free polytopes that safely constrain downstream MPC and trajectory optimization. We mathematically guarantee that the algorithm terminates in finite steps while ensuring continuous progress along any obstacle-free reference path. Extensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world LiDAR datasets demonstrate an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable corridor volumes. The complete pipeline is validated via high-fidelity quadrotor simulations and physical deployment on a quadrupedal robot navigating constrained environments using live LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [117] [Search-Aided Joint Agent-Environment Reinforcement Learning for Robust Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding with Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05588)
*He Jiang,Jingtian Yan,Yulun Zhang,Yimin Tang,Tanishq Duhan,Rishi Veerapaneni,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对终身多智能体路径规划（LMAPF）中现实运动约束（如安全约束和原地旋转）带来的协调难题，提出搜索辅助联合强化学习方法（SJRL）。该方法通过结合单步搜索规划器与联合优化智能体/环境策略的强化学习，在高密度地图和混合现实仓库中实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的LMAPF规划器多采用简化的运动学假设，忽略了现实应用（如自动化仓库）中的关键运动约束（鲁棒安全约束和原地旋转约束）。这些约束在高度受限空间中会大幅增加多智能体协调的难度，因此需要研究更现实的LMAPF模型和对应算法。

Method: 提出SJRL方法：1. 用Causal PIBT（一种单步搜索规划器）增强神经策略，以解决智能体碰撞并传播意图；2. 引入统一强化学习框架，联合优化智能体策略和环境策略，其中环境策略学习图边成本，通过反向Dijkstra搜索为智能体提供全局运动指导。

Result: 实验表明，SJRL在多个高密度地图上显著优于强基线Causal-PIBT。此外，在包含8个物理机器人和248个虚拟机器人的混合现实仓库环境中验证了方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: SJRL通过融合搜索方法与联合强化学习，成功应对了LMAPF-R2模型中的现实约束挑战，在复杂环境中实现了高效协调，为实际应用中的终身多智能体路径规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (LMAPF) requires repeatedly planning collision-free paths for agents that continuously receive new goals upon reaching their current ones. While many learning-based planners have been proposed for LMAPF, most rely on oversimplified kinematic assumptions that may overlook motion constraints critical to real-world performance. In this work, we study a more realistic LMAPF model derived from many real-world automated warehouse systems, termed LMAPF-R2, which incorporates robust safety constraints and in-place rotation constraints. These constraints substantially increase coordination difficulty, particularly in highly constrained spaces. To address these challenges, we propose Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL). We first augment neural policies with Causal PIBT, a single-step search-based planner that resolves agents' collisions and propagates their intentions. We then introduce a unified RL formulation that jointly optimizes agent and environment policies, where the environment policy learns graph edge costs to provide global movement guidance via backward Dijkstra search. Experiments demonstrate that SJRL achieves significant improvements over the strong search-based planner, Causal-PIBT, across multiple high-density maps. We further validate SJRL in a challenging mixed-reality warehouse environment with 8 physical robots and 248 virtual robots.

</details>


### [118] [KILVO: Kinematic-Inertial-LiDAR-Visual Odometry with Robust Multimodal Adaptation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05647)
*Jixin Gao,Fucheng Liu,Teng Zhang,Fusheng Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个专为人形机器人设计的多传感器融合里程计系统KILVO，该系统在一个混合滤波框架内高效融合关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR和相机数据，实现高精度、高效率且鲁棒的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在动态环境中执行任务时需要精确、实时的状态估计（里程计）。其平台通常装配多种传感器，但现有方法可能未充分利用这些传感器，或未充分适应人形机器人特有的运动学、接触条件以及可能出现的传感器降级或故障问题。因此，需要一个专为该平台设计、能有效融合多源异步数据并具有强鲁棒性的里程计框架。

Method: 系统核心是一个异步-顺序混合的误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器。具体方法包括：1. 惯性数据用于状态预测；2. 腿部运动学数据以高频率异步处理，提供本体感觉约束；3. 外部感知（LiDAR和相机）数据进行顺序更新：先通过点云配准提供几何先验，再通过光度误差进行视觉更新。框架具备多模态自适应能力以应对传感器故障，并集成了一个紧凑的接触估计模块，无需额外传感器即可与状态估计共享信息。

Result: 在多个公开数据集和真实世界场景中，针对不同人形机器人和步态模式进行的广泛实验表明，KILVO在精度、效率和输出频率方面都具有高度竞争力，并且对传感器性能下降和故障表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: KILVO框架通过精心设计的多传感器融合策略和鲁棒性机制，证明了其比现有的先进融合方法更适合人形机器人的实际应用需求。相关代码和数据集已在GitHub上开源。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>


### [119] [JoyAI-RA 0.5: Scaling Robot Manipulation Learning via Dual Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05674)
*JoyAI-RA Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了JoyAI-RA 0.5框架，这是一个视觉-语言-世界-动作通用模型，通过双动作对齐机制有效融合人类自我中心视频、仿真和真实机器人数据等异构来源，从而实现机器人操作学习的规模化扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据稀缺，通用策略需要从监督信号和具身体验各异的异构数据源学习，但这些数据的动作标签可能缺失或相互冲突。人类自我中心视频数据规模庞大，但与机器人数据差异显著，简单合并会导致负迁移而非知识共享。

Method: 提出JoyAI-RA 0.5框架，结合物理世界动力学先验和视觉语义，通过双动作对齐扩展操作学习：1）隐式动作对齐：从视觉转换推断潜在动作，使无动作标签的人类、仿真和机器人数据能指导潜在动作条件下的世界模型学习物理动力学；2）显式动作对齐：通过规范化动作表示和相机坐标系下的末端执行器分块相对动作，将可靠的人类和机器人轨迹统一到物理动作空间；3）内外循环强化学习阶段：将高效的任务适应与基础策略改进相结合。

Result: 在真实世界AgiBot基准测试中，JoyAI-RA对已见任务和未见变体均表现出强大性能。随着人类自我中心预训练数据量的增加，任务得分持续提升，且在最大规模数据下未出现平台期。

Conclusion: 丰富但弱标注的人类经验可以转化为可迁移的训练信号，使人类视频不再仅仅是弱辅助数据源，而是扩展操作能力的主要轴线。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

</details>


### [120] [Nonvisual Classification of Ground-Condition by Artificial Proprioception in an Amoeba-Inspired Autonomous Walking Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05684)
*Hyoto Yamaguchi,Zenji Yatabe,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy. We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

</details>


### [121] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）作为机器人规划器时面临的物理提示注入攻击。作者提出了一个包含“间接标识、任务重定义、权威冒充、冲突注入”四类攻击的分类法，并构建了一个包含20个攻击提示的基准测试。实验在三个前沿VLM（GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B）上进行评估，发现攻击成功率显著，且权威冒充和否定攻击具有跨模型迁移性。研究还分析了模型的推理轨迹，并评估了三种简单有效的防御措施，其中预处理文本遮罩方法达到了100%的有效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型在机器人系统中被越来越多地用作规划器，将自然语言指令转化为基于视觉场景理解的可执行动作，这种紧密耦合引入了新的攻击面：机器人视觉范围内的对抗性文本可以作为对VLM推理栈的间接提示注入。然而，这一新兴威胁尚未得到系统性的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，首次对VLM控制的机器人系统面临的物理提示注入攻击进行系统分析，以揭示其脆弱性并探索有效的防御方法。

Method: 研究采用了一种系统性的实验方法。首先，提出了一个四类攻击分类法（间接标识、任务重定义、权威冒充、冲突注入），并基于此构建了包含20个攻击提示的基准测试集。其次，在三个不同的物理场景布局中，使用三种不同表述（在目的地具体性和规则明确性上不同）的合法指令，对三个前沿VLM模型进行了评估。实验共进行了5,670次试验。此外，通过分析模型的推理轨迹，研究了攻击成功的机制。最后，评估了三种防御策略：基于提示的防御、两阶段验证和预处理文本遮罩。

Result: 实验结果显示，VLM控制的系统对物理提示注入攻击是脆弱的。不同模型的攻击成功率存在差异：GPT-4o为27.0%，Gemini 2.5 Flash为29.4%，Qwen3-VL-32B为5.0%。权威冒充和否定攻击在所有三个模型中都表现出迁移性。对推理轨迹的分析表明，成功的攻击几乎都是被模型有意识地（99.9%的确认率）接受的。不同模型展现出结构不同的防御机制，例如Gemini通过显式拒绝，而GPT-4o则表现出感知上的疏忽。三种防御措施均能有效降低风险：基于提示的防御有效率为75-100%，两阶段验证为85-100%，而预处理文本遮罩达到了100%的有效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，VLM控制的机器人操作任务确实容易受到人类可读的物理标识的提示注入攻击，构成了有意义的安全风险。然而，简单的防御措施能够大幅降低这种风险。防御策略的选择存在权衡：所评估的防御措施在保留基准任务通用能力的同时，可能会损害那些需要读取场景内标签的任务。这项工作强调了在将VLM部署于物理机器人系统时，必须将对抗性物理输入的鲁棒性作为关键设计考虑因素。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs. The defenses preserve general task capabilities in our benchmark, but they may impair tasks that require reading in-scene labels.

</details>


### [122] [ATP: Anatomical Torque with Passivity-based Control Framework for Safe Upper-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05723)
*Yu Chen,Gong Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ATP方法，用于安全、通用的上肢外骨骼辅助，通过可扩展的肌骨模拟框架、在线扭矩优化和无源控制器实现准确且舒适的辅助，并在实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖知识辅助的外骨骼能提供跨任务的响应式支持，但目前研究主要集中在下肢外骨骼，其周期性承重运动对扭矩精度要求较低。将这种辅助扩展到复杂、非周期性的上肢运动仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出基于无源控制的解剖扭矩方法：1. 可扩展的肌骨模拟框架，训练统一的强化学习肌肉控制器，生成解剖参考扭矩；2. 在线扭矩优化方案，适应多样运动并抑制异常峰值；3. 交互扭矩控制器，通过缆索驱动的柔性外骨骼提供辅助，并利用能量罐理论保证系统无源性。

Result: 仿真实验和现实世界实验表明，该方法能准确追踪长时间运动序列，并泛化到实时人体运动。控制器实现精确扭矩追踪并保持无源性，在能量补充后恢复追踪。五名参与者的肌电图研究显示，与重力补偿和开环辅助相比，目标肌肉活动在静态和动态任务中减少，动态多关节任务中减少高达48%。

Conclusion: ATP方法通过整合肌骨模拟、在线优化和无源控制，为上肢外骨骼提供了一种安全、舒适且准确的辅助方案，能有效泛化到多样化的非周期性上肢运动，并显著降低肌肉负荷。

Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>


### [123] [Near-sensor Computing for Rapid Visuotactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05725)
*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出近端计算框架，通过专用硬件管线实现实时触觉几何重建，显著降低延迟并提高能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器依赖主机处理重建接触几何，导致功耗高、数据传输延迟大且调度延迟不确定，限制了机器人系统的感知与响应速度。

Method: 实现了一个近端计算框架，其核心是一个采用全流式硬件管线的谱泊松求解器，无需数据依赖分支或迭代收敛，提供确定性延迟。

Result: 计算核心功耗约347mW，工作频率166MHz，重建128x128帧的深度值固定延迟为0.211ms。在15种接触几何测试中，重建深度与双精度参考值仅相差峰值深度的0.17%。基于此重建的片上决策，将机器人保护反射环路的响应时间缩短至28.3±4.9ms（主机方案为169.9±27.8ms）。

Conclusion: 近端重建方案能够在满足机器人快速接触响应需求的时间尺度上，提供精确、高能效且确定性的触觉几何信息。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors reconstruct dense contact geometry from measured surface gradients, but host-based processing increases power consumption and introduces data-transfer delays and variable scheduling latency, limiting the sensing and response speed of robotic systems. To address these limitations, we implement a near-sensor computing framework that includes a spectral Poisson solver as a fully streaming hardware pipeline. The computational core logic has an estimated power consumption of 347 mW and achieves high throughput without data-dependent branching or iterative convergence, thereby providing deterministic latency. Operating at 166 MHz, the pipeline produces the first depth value of each 128x128 frame 35,107 cycles after receiving the first input pixel, corresponding to a fixed latency of 0.211 ms. Across 15 contact geometries, the reconstructed depths differ from a double-precision reference by 0.17 % of the peak contact depth. On-chip decisions based on these reconstructions close a robot protective reflex loop in 28.3 +/- 4.9 ms, compared with 169.9 +/- 27.8 ms for an equivalent host-based loop using the same actuator. These results demonstrate that near-sensor reconstruction can provide accurate, energy-efficient, and deterministic tactile geometry on timescales suitable for rapid robotic contact responses.

</details>


### [124] [In-Context VLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Language via In-Context Post-Training and Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05738)
*Jiarui Yang,Wen Huang,Jiale Zhang,Maowei Hu,Hang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为\ourmethod{}的新框架，用于改进视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的泛化操作能力。该框架通过上下文后训练和智能体工具使用接口，使模型能够消费和理解基于感知证据的语言指令，而非生成语言。实验表明，在多个仿真和真实世界任务中，该方法在性能和效率上均优于基于文本链式思考的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖行为克隆（模仿专家动作块），而引入文本链式思考（CoT）进行推理会带来问题：自由形式的文本推理缺乏 grounded、延迟破坏闭环时序、且推理与动作的优化目标冲突，导致策略学习“叙述”而非“行动”。因此，VLA需要的是消费 grounded 语言的能力，而非生成语言的能力。

Method: 引入\ourmethod{}框架，核心包含两个机制：(1) 上下文后训练：将感知证据作为结构化上下文注入模型，仅对动作进行监督，使模型学习理解多样化的语言描述。(2) 智能体工具使用接口：策略可以主动查询开放词汇检测器、单目深度估计和视觉-语言模型等工具，以获取任务相关信息。该框架的数据引擎能够生成多样化、可改述的、基于证据的空间描述。

Result: 在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv和LIBERO仿真基准测试，以及8项真实世界机器人操作任务上进行评估。结果表明，\ourmethod{}在匹配的配置下，相较于基于CoT的方法，在性能和效率上均达到了最先进的水平。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在VLA模型中，盲目引入自由形式的文本链式思考会损害低级控制性能。所提出的\ourmethod{}框架通过将模型设计为语言消费者而非生成者，并利用上下文学习和工具使用接口来增强其理解和利用语言的能力，成功解决了这一问题，在多项任务中实现了优越的性能和效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the dominant recipe for generalist manipulation, yet they are almost universally trained by behavior cloning: a policy imitates expert action chunks conditioned on a static image and a fixed instruction. A natural remedy is to inject explicit reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT). We show, both empirically and analytically, that free-form textual CoT degrades low-level control: the reasoning it produces is ungrounded, its latency breaks closed-loop timing, and, crucially, the reasoning and action tokens are optimized against conflicting objectives so that the policy learns to narrate rather than to act. We argue that what a VLA needs is not the ability to generate language, but the ability to consume grounded language. To this end we introduce \textbf{\ourmethod{}}, a framework that endows a VLA with language competence through (i) in-context post-training, in which perceptual evidence is injected as structured context and the model is supervised only on actions, and (ii) an agentic tool-use interface, in which the policy queries open-vocabulary detectors, monocular depth, and a vision--language model to actively acquire task-relevant information. Rather than emitting a single templated caption, our data engine produces diverse, paraphrased, and evidence-conditioned spatial descriptions, so that the policy learns to interpret language it has never seen verbatim. Across the RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO simulation benchmarks, together with 8 real-world robot manipulation tasks, our method consistently achieves SOTA results in both performance and efficiency when compared with CoT-based approaches under matched configurations.

</details>


### [125] [Acoustic-driven millimetric helical robot: ultrasonic synergistic manipulation in confined fluidic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05746)
*Hanlin Wang,Xin Wang,Xinwei Wei,Jiaxu Liu,Le Wang,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种协同多声场方法，利用声辐射力与声流的协同作用，显著提升了毫米级螺旋机器人在受限生物环境中的推进效率与机动性，并通过仿真与实验验证了其导航、爬坡、垂直运动等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的声场操控技术在微纳尺度物体操控上已趋成熟，但其向毫米级机器人的扩展受限于在复杂生物环境中推进效率不足的问题，限制了在生物医学领域的应用潜力。

Method: 1. 采用多物理场仿真模拟毫米级螺旋机器人在组合声场下的动力学行为。2. 设计实验验证机器人的运动性能，包括平面导航、倾斜攀爬和垂直运动。3. 通过半自主导航实验测试超声波协同对机动性的提升效果。4. 在体外猪静脉血管模型中测试机器人在生物受限环境下的单向及往复运动能力。

Result: 1. 仿真与实验共同证实，协同声场能有效驱动毫米级螺旋机器人实现可控运动。2. 机器人展现出平面导航、爬坡、垂直运动等多种运动模式。3. 超声波协同作用显著提升了机器人的操控性和机动性。4. 在模拟生物血管的受限环境中，机器人可实现稳定的单向和往复运动。

Conclusion: 本研究将声学微操控成功扩展至毫米尺度，阐明了其驱动机制，为在复杂生物环境中实现机器人灵活、可控运动提供了新技术路径，有望推动相关生物医学应用的发展。

Abstract: Acoustic field-driven manipulation provides a non-contact and non-invasive strategy for controlling microscale and nanoscale objects, yet its extension to millimeter-scale robots was limited by insufficient propulsion efficiency in confined biological environments. Here, a coordinated multi-acoustic-field approach is introduced, which harnesses the synergistic action of acoustic radiation forces and acoustic streaming flows to enable controlled locomotion of millimeter-scale helical robots and enhance propulsion. Multiphysics simulations captured the dynamics of millimeter-scale helical robots under combined acoustic fields, and experimental validation demonstrated their locomotion capabilities, including planar navigation, inclined climbing, and vertical motion. Semi-autonomous navigation experiments further confirmed that ultrasonic synergy substantially improved maneuverability. In vitro tests in porcine venous vessels demonstrated that coordinated acoustic fields supported both unidirectional and reciprocating motion under biologically relevant confinement. These findings provide mechanistic insight into scaling acoustic micromanipulation to the millimetre regime and support biomedical applications requiring versatile and controllable robotic mobility.

</details>


### [126] [XEWorld: Can Action-Conditioned World Models Generalize to Unseen Robot Embodiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05799)
*Yixiang Chen,Jiabing Yang,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Keji He,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Ziheng He,Xiangnan Wu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed
